\StringTok{    function triggerFileUpload()\{}
\StringTok{      var w=document.getElementById(\textquotesingle{}company\_file\textquotesingle{});if(!w)return;}
\StringTok{      var r=w.querySelector(\textquotesingle{}input[type=file]\textquotesingle{});if(r)r.click();else w.click();}
\StringTok{    \}}
\StringTok{    function highlightNav(id)\{}
\StringTok{      [\textquotesingle{}nav{-}dashboard\textquotesingle{},\textquotesingle{}nav{-}po\textquotesingle{},\textquotesingle{}nav{-}grn\textquotesingle{},\textquotesingle{}nav{-}reports\textquotesingle{}].forEach(function(n)\{}
\StringTok{        var el=document.getElementById(n);if(!el)return;}
\StringTok{        if(n===id)\{el.style.background=\textquotesingle{}\#da191e\textquotesingle{};el.style.color=\textquotesingle{}white\textquotesingle{};\}}
\StringTok{        else\{el.style.background=\textquotesingle{}\textquotesingle{};el.style.color=\textquotesingle{}\#64748b\textquotesingle{};\}}
\StringTok{      \});}
\StringTok{    \}}

\NormalTok{      dt}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{x}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{options}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{initComplete }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ htmlwidgets}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{JS}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sprintf}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"function(settings, json) \{}
\StringTok{        var api = this.api();}
\StringTok{        var months = \%s;}
\StringTok{        var headerRow = \textquotesingle{}\textless{}tr style=}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{background:\#f1f5f9;border{-}bottom:2px solid \#da191e;}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{};}
\StringTok{        headerRow += \textquotesingle{}\textless{}th style=}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{padding:8px;text{-}align:left;font{-}weight:700;font{-}size:11px;}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{\textgreater{}Company ID\textless{}/th\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{};}
\StringTok{        headerRow += \textquotesingle{}\textless{}th style=}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{padding:8px;text{-}align:left;font{-}weight:700;font{-}size:11px;}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{\textgreater{}Company Name\textless{}/th\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{};}
\StringTok{        months.forEach(function(m) \{}
\StringTok{          var d = new Date(m + \textquotesingle{}{-}01\textquotesingle{});}
\StringTok{          var monthName = d.toLocaleDateString(\textquotesingle{}en{-}US\textquotesingle{}, \{month: \textquotesingle{}short\textquotesingle{}, year: \textquotesingle{}numeric\textquotesingle{}\});}
\StringTok{          headerRow += \textquotesingle{}\textless{}th colspan=}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{5}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{ style=}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{padding:8px;text{-}align:center;font{-}weight:700;font{-}size:12px;color:\#0e1d28;border{-}right:1px solid \#e2e8f0;}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{} + monthName + \textquotesingle{}\textless{}/th\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{};}
\StringTok{        \});}
\StringTok{        headerRow += \textquotesingle{}\textless{}th colspan=}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{5}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{ style=}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{padding:8px;text{-}align:center;font{-}weight:700;font{-}size:12px;color:\#0e1d28;background:\#f0f9ff;}\SpecialCharTok{\textbackslash{}"}\StringTok{\textgreater{}OVERALL TOTALS\textless{}/th\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{};}
\StringTok{        headerRow += \textquotesingle{}\textless{}/tr\textgreater{}\textquotesingle{};}
\StringTok{        var thead = api.table().header();}
\StringTok{        $(thead).find(\textquotesingle{}tr\textquotesingle{}).first().before(headerRow);}
\StringTok{      \}"}\NormalTok{, jsonlite}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{toJSON}\NormalTok{(months)))}
